==== dump ast examples/api.fung
-- stdout
| (program (import http) (import json) (import time) (fn json_response(status, data) (block (map (entry status status) (entry headers (map (entry content-type "application/json"))) (entry body (call (field stringify json) data))))) (fn health(started, requests) (block (call json_response 200 (map (entry ok true) (entry uptime (call str (binary - (call (field now time)) started))) (entry requests (call deref requests)))))) (fn echo(req) (block (match (call (field parse json) (field body req)) (arm (ptag Ok (pbind data)) (nil) (block (call json_response 200 (map (entry received data) (entry at (call (field format time) (call (field now time)))))))) (arm (ptag Err (pbind e)) (nil) (block (map (entry status 400) (entry body (interp "bad json: " (field message e))))))))) (fn handler(started, requests) (block (fn (req) (block (call swap requests (fn (n) (block (binary + n 1)))) (match (tuple (field method req) (field path req)) (arm (ptuple (plit "GET") (plit "/health")) (nil) (block (call health started requests))) (arm (ptuple (plit "POST") (plit "/echo")) (nil) (block (call echo req))) (arm (pwild) (nil) (block (map (entry status 404) (entry body (interp "not found: " (field path req))))))))))) (test "health, echo and 404" (block (assign port (call channel 1)) (assign h (call handler (call (field now time)) (call agent 0))) (assign s (call spawn (fn () (block (call (field serve http) "127.0.0.1:0" h (named ready (fn (a) (block (call send port a))))))))) (assign base (binary + "http://" (unwrap ! (call recv port)))) (assign r (unwrap ! (call (field get http) (interp base "/health")))) (assert (binary == (field status r) 200)) (assert (binary == (field ok (unwrap ! (call (field parse json) (field body r)))) true)) (assign e (unwrap ! (call (field post http) (interp base "/echo") (call (field stringify json) (map (entry x 1)))))) (assert (binary == (field x (field received (unwrap ! (call (field parse json) (field body e))))) 1)) (assert (binary == (field status (unwrap ! (call (field post http) (interp base "/echo") "{"))) 400)) (assert (binary == (field status (unwrap ! (call (field get http) (interp base "/nope")))) 404)) (assert (binary == (field requests (unwrap ! (call (field parse json) (field body (unwrap ! (call (field get http) (interp base "/health"))))))) 5)) (call cancel s))))

(call (field walk fs) dir))) (assign sized (pipe files (call map (fn (p) (block (tuple p (call get (coalesce (call (field stat fs) p) (map)) "size" 0))))))) (pipe (pipe sized (call sort_by (fn (e) (block (match e (arm (ptuple (pwild) (pbind s)) (nil) (block (unary - s)))))))) (call take n)))) (fn human(n) (block (then (binary < n 1024) (interp n "B") (then (binary < n (binary * 1024 1024)) (interp (binary / (binary // (binary * n 10) 1024) 10.0) "K") (interp (binary / (binary // (binary * n 10) (binary * 1024 1024)) 10.0) "M"))))) (test "human sizes" (block (assert (binary == (call human 10) "10B")) (assert (binary == (call human 1536) "1.5K")) (assert (binary == (call human (binary * (binary * 3 1024) 1024)) "3.0M")))) (test "largest keeps order and count" (block (call (field with_temp_dir fs) (fn (d) (block (unwrap ! (call (field write fs) (call (field join path) d "a") (call repeat "x" 10))) (unwrap ! (call (field write fs) (call (field join path) d "b") (call repeat "x" 30))) (unwrap ! | (program (fn parse(text) (block (assign rows (pipe (pipe (call lines text) (call map trim)) (call filter (fn (l) (block (binary and (binary != l "") (unary not (call starts_with l "#")))))))) (call fold rows (map) (fn (acc, line) (block (assign (ptuple (pbind k) (pbind v)) (match (call split line "=") (arm (plist (pbind k) (prest rest)) (nil) (block (tuple (call trim (call replace k "export " "")) (call trim (call join rest "="))))))) (call put acc k (call trim (call trim v "\"") "'"))))))) (fn problems(cfg, required, ports) (block (assign missing (pipe (call filter required (fn (k) (block (binary == (call get cfg k "") "")))) (call map (fn (k) (block (interp "missing: " k)))))) (assign bad_ports (pipe (call filter ports (fn (k) (block (binary and (call contains cfg k) (unary not (call valid_port (call get cfg k))))))) (call map (fn (k) (block (interp "not a port: " k "=" (call get cfg k))))))) (binary + missing bad_ports))) (fn valid_port(s) (block (match (call parse_int s) (arm (ptag Ok (pbind n)) (nil) (block (binary and (binary > n 0) (binary < n 65536)))) (arm (pwild) (nil) (block false))))) (test "parse handles comments, export and quotes" (block (assign cfg (call parse "# db\nexport DB_HOST=\"localhost\"\nDB_PORT=5432\n\nTOKEN='a=b=c'\n")) (assert (binary == cfg (map (entry DB_HOST "localhost") (entry DB_PORT "5432") (entry TOKEN "a=b=c")))))) (test "problems reports missing keys and bad ports" (block (assign cfg (call parse "DB_HOST=x\nDB_PORT=99999\nEMPTY=\n")) (assert (binary == (call problems cfg (list "DB_HOST" "EMPTY" "SECRET") (list "DB_PORT")) (list "missing: EMPTY" "missing: SECRET" "not a port: DB_PORT=99999"))) (assert (binary == (call problems (call parse "DB_PORT=80") (list) (list "DB_PORT")) (list))))))

==== dump ast examples/envcheck.fung
-- stdout
| (program (import fs) (import cli) (import "./dotenv" (parse, problems)) (assign opts (call (field parse cli) args)) (assign file (coalesce (call first (field rest opts)) (call fail "usage: envcheck.fung <file> KEY... [--ports A,B]"))) (assign required (call drop (field rest opts) 1)) (assign ports (pipe (call split (call get (field flags opts) "ports" "") ",") (call filter (fn (p) (block (binary != p "")))))) (assign found (call problems (call parse (unwrap ! (call (field read fs) file))) required ports)) (call each found (fn (p) (block (call eprint p)))) (if (unary not (call is_empty found)) (block (call exit 1)) (nil)) (call print "ok:" file))

==== dump ast examples/kebab.fung
-- stdout
| (program (import path) (fn kebab(name) (block (assign kept (for (pbind c) (call chars (call lower name)) (block (then (binary in c "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789") c "-")))) (pipe (pipe (pipe (call join kept) (call split "-")) (call filter (fn (p) (block (binary != p ""))))) (call join "-")))) (fn target(file) (block (assign stem (call kebab (call (field stem path) file))) (then (binary == stem "") file (binary + stem (call lower (call (field ext path) file)))))) (test "kebab squeezes and trims" (block (assert (binary == (call kebab "My Report (final)") "my-report-final")) (assert (binary == (call kebab "--a--b--") "a-b")) (assert (binary == (call kebab "참치") "")))) (test "target keeps names without ascii letters" (block (assert (binary == (call target "My File (1).TXT") "my-file-1.txt")) (assert (binary == (call target "참치.md") "참치.md")) (assert (binary == (call target "already-ok.txt") "already-ok.txt")))))

1]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]]
